I can provide you with the information you requested.

**Summary of Outages**

The outages affected Google Cloud Service (GCS) and Apigee services in various regions.

**Outage Details per Cloud Provider**

### Google Cloud Service (GCS)

* **Apigee**
	+ Region: asia-east1
	+ Availability Zone: Not specified
	+ Impact Date: 2024-04-23 12:25 UTC
	+ Duration: 2 hours and 18 minutes
	+ Root Cause: Impacted customers may receive HTML errors instead of correct data while trying to access Apigee portal.
	+ Downtime Period: 2024-04-23 14:24 - 15:53 UTC
	+ Resolved Date: 2024-04-23 15:15 UTC
